Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a mass_spec algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 138. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 56/2.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_138(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 138. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^56(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 216.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Under the optimality condition assumption on mass_spec structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 793. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 429 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 793 is immediate from the optimality condition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the mass_spec structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 429. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
